# ФАЙЛ [7/36]: backend\networkx_models.py
# Размер: 427 символов, 15 строк
######################################################################

import networkx as nx
import numpy as np

def generate_er(N: int, p: float):
    G = nx.erdos_renyi_graph(N, p)
    nodes = np.random.uniform(0, 1, (N, 3)).tolist()
    links = list(G.edges())
    return nodes, links

def generate_ba(N: int, m: int):
    if m >= N:
        m = N - 1
    G = nx.barabasi_albert_graph(N, m)
    nodes = np.random.uniform(0, 1, (N, 3)).tolist()
    links = list(G.edges())
    return nodes, links


######################################################################
# ФАЙЛ [8/36]: backend\randLPN_CPP.cpp
# Размер: 19711 символов, 568 строк
######################################################################

//Python module to generate linear physical networks
#define PY_SSIZE_T_CLEAN
#include <Python.h>
#include <iostream>
#include <chrono>
#include <random>
#include <CGAL/Simple_cartesian.h>

#define SMALL_NUMBER 1e-14
#define MAX_NODE_TRIAL 100000
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846

typedef CGAL::Simple_cartesian<double> Kernel;
typedef Kernel::Point_3 PT;
typedef Kernel::Segment_3 SEG;

// construct a trivial random generator engine from a time-based seed:
unsigned seed = static_cast<unsigned>(std::chrono::system_clock::now().time_since_epoch().count());
std::default_random_engine generator(seed);

template <class GEOM1, class GEOM2>
bool DistTest(double squared_dist, GEOM1& geom, std::vector<GEOM2> &geoms) {
    for (GEOM2 geom2: geoms){
        double sd = CGAL::squared_distance(geom, geom2);
        if ((sd > SMALL_NUMBER) && (sd < squared_dist)){ // questionable practice
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

// Physical ER - размещение точек с проверкой минимального расстояния
int RandPointCloud(int N, double dist, std::vector<PT> &points){
    int max_node_trial = MAX_NODE_TRIAL;
    double squared_dist = dist * dist;
    PT point;
    std::uniform_real_distribution<double> distribution(0.0, 1.0);

    int n;
    for(n = 0; n < N; n++){
        int trial = 0;
        do{
            point = PT(distribution(generator), distribution(generator), distribution(generator));
            trial++;
        } while((!DistTest<PT,PT>(squared_dist, point, points)) && (trial < max_node_trial));

        if (trial < max_node_trial){
            points.push_back(point);
        } else {
            n--;
            break;
        }
    }
    return n;
}

// Physical ER - полная генерация (все три ограничения: связь-связь, связь-вершина, вершина-вершина)
int LinPhysER_C(int N, int L, double dist, std::vector<PT> &points, std::vector<std::array<int,2>> &vws, std::vector<int> &t_add){
    int n = RandPointCloud(N, dist, points);
    double squared_dist = dist * dist;

    // edges are attempted to be added in random order
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> order;
    order.reserve(n * (n - 1) / 2);
    for(int v = 0; v < n; v++)
        for(int w = v + 1; w < n; w++)
            order.push_back({v, w});
    std::shuffle(std::begin(order), std::end(order), generator);

    // let's get ready to add those edges
    int l = 0, t = 0;
    std::vector<SEG> segs;
    segs.reserve(L);
    vws.reserve(L);
    t_add.reserve(L);

    // let's add those edges
    for (auto vw: order){
        SEG seg(points[vw[0]], points[vw[1]]);
        if (DistTest<SEG,PT>(squared_dist, seg, points) && DistTest<SEG,SEG>(squared_dist, seg, segs)){
            segs.push_back(seg);
            vws.push_back({vw[0], vw[1]});
            t_add.push_back(t);
            l++;
            if (l == L) break;
        }
        t++;
    }
    return n;
}

// only considering link-link interaction
// nodes are still placed at least lambda (=dist) away from each other
int LinPhysER_linkonly_C(int N, int L, double dist, std::vector<PT> &points, std::vector<std::array<int,2>> &vws, std::vector<int> &t_add){
    double squared_dist = dist * dist;
    std::uniform_real_distribution<double> distribution(0.0, 1.0);
    for(int n = 0; n < N; n++)
        points.push_back(PT(distribution(generator), distribution(generator), distribution(generator)));

    // edges are attempted to be added in random order
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> order;
    order.reserve(N * (N - 1) / 2);
    for(int v = 0; v < N; v++)
        for(int w = v + 1; w < N; w++)
            order.push_back({v, w});
    std::shuffle(std::begin(order), std::end(order), generator);

    // let's get ready to add those edges
    int l = 0, t = 0;
    std::vector<SEG> segs;
    segs.reserve(L);
    vws.reserve(L);
    t_add.reserve(L);

    // let's add those edges
    for (auto vw: order){
        SEG seg(points[vw[0]], points[vw[1]]);
        if (DistTest<SEG,SEG>(squared_dist, seg, segs)){
            segs.push_back(seg);
            vws.push_back({vw[0], vw[1]});
            t_add.push_back(t);
            l++;
            if (l == L) break;
        }
        t++;
    }
    return N;
}

// НОВАЯ ОБОБЩЁННАЯ ФУНКЦИЯ: настраиваемые физические ограничения
// check_node_node: если true, проверяется расстояние между узлами (физическое ограничение 3)
// check_node_link: если true, проверяется пересечение связей с узлами (физическое ограничение 2)
// Физическое ограничение 1 (связь-связь) проверяется всегда
int LinPhysER_general_C(int N, int L, double dist,
                        std::vector<PT> &points,
                        std::vector<std::array<int,2>> &vws,
                        std::vector<int> &t_add,
                        bool check_node_node, bool check_node_link){
    double squared_dist = dist * dist;

    // Размещение узлов
    if (check_node_node) {
        // С проверкой расстояний между узлами (физическое ограничение 3)
        RandPointCloud(N, dist, points);
    } else {
        // Без проверки — просто случайные точки
        std::uniform_real_distribution<double> distribution(0.0, 1.0);
        for (int n = 0; n < N; n++)
            points.push_back(PT(distribution(generator), distribution(generator), distribution(generator)));
    }

    int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());

    // Случайный порядок кандидатов в рёбра
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> order;
    order.reserve(n * (n - 1) / 2);
    for(int v = 0; v < n; v++)
        for(int w = v + 1; w < n; w++)
            order.push_back({v, w});
    std::shuffle(std::begin(order), std::end(order), generator);

    // Добавление рёбер
    int l = 0, t = 0;
    std::vector<SEG> segs;
    segs.reserve(L);
    vws.reserve(L);
    t_add.reserve(L);

    for (auto vw: order){
        SEG seg(points[vw[0]], points[vw[1]]);
        bool ok = true;

        // Проверка связь-вершина (физическое ограничение 2)
        if (check_node_link) {
            ok = ok && DistTest<SEG,PT>(squared_dist, seg, points);
        }

        // Проверка связь-связь (физическое ограничение 1) — всегда
        ok = ok && DistTest<SEG,SEG>(squared_dist, seg, segs);

        if (ok) {
            segs.push_back(seg);
            vws.push_back({vw[0], vw[1]});
            t_add.push_back(t);
            l++;
            if (l == L) break;
        }
        t++;
    }

    return n;
}

// НОВАЯ ФУНКЦИЯ: принимает точки из Python (для поддержки разных распределений)
// points_in: входные точки из Python
int LinPhysER_general_with_points_C(int L, double dist,
                                     const std::vector<PT> &points_in,
                                     std::vector<std::array<int,2>> &vws,
                                     std::vector<int> &t_add,
                                     bool check_node_node, bool check_node_link){
    double squared_dist = dist * dist;
    int n = static_cast<int>(points_in.size());

    // Копируем входные точки
    std::vector<PT> points = points_in;

    // Если нужна проверка расстояний между узлами, фильтруем точки
    if (check_node_node) {
        std::vector<PT> filtered_points;
        filtered_points.reserve(n);

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            bool valid = true;
            for (const PT& existing : filtered_points) {
                if (CGAL::squared_distance(points[i], existing) < squared_dist) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (valid) {
                filtered_points.push_back(points[i]);
            }
        }
        points = filtered_points;
        n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    }

    // Случайный порядок кандидатов в рёбра
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> order;
    order.reserve(n * (n - 1) / 2);
    for(int v = 0; v < n; v++)
        for(int w = v + 1; w < n; w++)
            order.push_back({v, w});
    std::shuffle(std::begin(order), std::end(order), generator);

    // Добавление рёбер
    int l = 0, t = 0;
    std::vector<SEG> segs;
    segs.reserve(L);
    vws.reserve(L);
    t_add.reserve(L);

    for (auto vw: order){
        SEG seg(points[vw[0]], points[vw[1]]);
        bool ok = true;

        // Проверка связь-вершина
        if (check_node_link) {
            ok = ok && DistTest<SEG,PT>(squared_dist, seg, points);
        }

        // Проверка связь-связь
        ok = ok && DistTest<SEG,SEG>(squared_dist, seg, segs);

        if (ok) {
            segs.push_back(seg);
            vws.push_back({vw[0], vw[1]});
            t_add.push_back(t);
            l++;
            if (l == L) break;
        }
        t++;
    }

    return n;
}

// distance for all pairs of segments upto a threshold
void AllToAllSegmentSquaredDist_C(int N, double maxsd, std::vector<PT> &points, std::vector<std::array<int,2>> &vws, std::vector<std::array<int,2>> &metaedges, std::vector<double> &squareddists){
    // pick random points
    std::uniform_real_distribution<double> distribution(0.0, 1.0);
    for(int n = 0; n < N; n++)
        points.push_back(PT(distribution(generator), distribution(generator), distribution(generator)));

    // create segment objects
    std::vector<SEG> segs;
    segs.reserve(N * (N - 1) / 2);
    vws.reserve(N * (N - 1) / 2);

    for(int v = 0; v < N; v++)
        for(int w = v + 1; w < N; w++){
            segs.push_back(SEG(points[v], points[w]));
            vws.push_back({v, w});
        }

    double sd;
    for(int s = 0; s < (int)vws.size(); s++)
        for(int t = s + 1; t < (int)vws.size(); t++){
            if(vws[s][0] != vws[t][0] && vws[s][0] != vws[t][1] && vws[s][1] != vws[t][0] && vws[s][1] != vws[t][1]){
                sd = CGAL::squared_distance(segs[s], segs[t]);
                if (sd < maxsd){
                    squareddists.push_back(sd);
                    metaedges.push_back({s, t});
                }
            }
        }
    return;
}

//****************************
// Exposing functions to python
//****************************

static PyObject* LinPhysER(PyObject* self, PyObject* args){
    int N, L;
    double d;
    if (!PyArg_ParseTuple(args, "iid", &N, &L, &d))
        return NULL;

    std::vector<PT> points;
    points.reserve(N);
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> vws;
    std::vector<int> t_add;

    LinPhysER_C(N, L, d, points, vws, t_add);

    PyObject* python_points = PyList_New(points.size());
    for (int v = 0; v < (int)points.size(); v++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_points, v, Py_BuildValue("(fff)", points[v].x(), points[v].y(), points[v].z()));

    PyObject* python_vws = PyList_New(vws.size());
    for (int e = 0; e < (int)vws.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_vws, e, Py_BuildValue("(ii)", vws[e][0], vws[e][1]));

    PyObject* python_t_add = PyList_New(t_add.size());
    for (int e = 0; e < (int)t_add.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_t_add, e, PyLong_FromLong(t_add[e]));

    PyObject* return_tuple = Py_BuildValue("(OOO)", python_points, python_vws, python_t_add);

    Py_DECREF(python_points);
    Py_DECREF(python_vws);
    Py_DECREF(python_t_add);

    return return_tuple;
}

static PyObject* LinPhysER_linkonly(PyObject* self, PyObject* args){
    int N, L;
    double d;
    if (!PyArg_ParseTuple(args, "iid", &N, &L, &d))
        return NULL;

    std::vector<PT> points;
    points.reserve(N);
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> vws;
    std::vector<int> t_add;

    LinPhysER_linkonly_C(N, L, d, points, vws, t_add);

    PyObject* python_points = PyList_New(points.size());
    for (int v = 0; v < (int)points.size(); v++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_points, v, Py_BuildValue("(fff)", points[v].x(), points[v].y(), points[v].z()));

    PyObject* python_vws = PyList_New(vws.size());
    for (int e = 0; e < (int)vws.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_vws, e, Py_BuildValue("(ii)", vws[e][0], vws[e][1]));

    PyObject* python_t_add = PyList_New(t_add.size());
    for (int e = 0; e < (int)t_add.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_t_add, e, PyLong_FromLong(t_add[e]));

    PyObject* return_tuple = Py_BuildValue("(OOO)", python_points, python_vws, python_t_add);

    Py_DECREF(python_points);
    Py_DECREF(python_vws);
    Py_DECREF(python_t_add);

    return return_tuple;
}

// НОВАЯ Python-обёртка для обобщённой функции
static PyObject* LinPhysER_general(PyObject* self, PyObject* args){
    int N, L;
    double d;
    int check_node_node, check_node_link;

    if (!PyArg_ParseTuple(args, "iidii", &N, &L, &d, &check_node_node, &check_node_link))
        return NULL;

    std::vector<PT> points;
    points.reserve(N);
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> vws;
    std::vector<int> t_add;

    LinPhysER_general_C(N, L, d, points, vws, t_add,
                        (bool)check_node_node, (bool)check_node_link);

    PyObject* python_points = PyList_New(points.size());
    for (int v = 0; v < (int)points.size(); v++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_points, v, Py_BuildValue("(fff)", points[v].x(), points[v].y(), points[v].z()));

    PyObject* python_vws = PyList_New(vws.size());
    for (int e = 0; e < (int)vws.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_vws, e, Py_BuildValue("(ii)", vws[e][0], vws[e][1]));

    PyObject* python_t_add = PyList_New(t_add.size());
    for (int e = 0; e < (int)t_add.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_t_add, e, PyLong_FromLong(t_add[e]));

    PyObject* return_tuple = Py_BuildValue("(OOO)", python_points, python_vws, python_t_add);

    Py_DECREF(python_points);
    Py_DECREF(python_vws);
    Py_DECREF(python_t_add);

    return return_tuple;
}

// НОВАЯ Python-обёртка для функции с внешними точками
// Принимает точки как список списков или список кортежей [[x,y,z], ...] или [(x,y,z), ...]
static PyObject* LinPhysER_general_with_points(PyObject* self, PyObject* args){
    int L;
    double d;
    PyObject* points_list;
    int check_node_node, check_node_link;
    
    if (!PyArg_ParseTuple(args, "idOii", &L, &d, &points_list, &check_node_node, &check_node_link))
        return NULL;
    
    // Проверяем, что аргумент - список
    if (!PyList_Check(points_list)) {
        PyErr_SetString(PyExc_TypeError, "Points must be a list of [x,y,z] coordinates");
        return NULL;
    }
    
    Py_ssize_t n_points = PyList_Size(points_list);
    std::vector<PT> points_in;
    points_in.reserve(n_points);
    
    for (Py_ssize_t i = 0; i < n_points; i++) {
        PyObject* point = PyList_GetItem(points_list, i);  // borrowed reference
        if (!point) {
            PyErr_SetString(PyExc_RuntimeError, "Failed to get point from list");
            return NULL;
        }
        
        // Проверяем, что point - последовательность (список или кортеж)
        if (!PySequence_Check(point)) {
            PyErr_SetString(PyExc_TypeError, "Each point must be a sequence [x,y,z]");
            return NULL;
        }
        
        Py_ssize_t coord_count = PySequence_Size(point);
        if (coord_count != 3) {
            PyErr_Format(PyExc_ValueError, "Each point must have 3 coordinates, got %zd", coord_count);
            return NULL;
        }
        
        // Используем PySequence_GetItem - работает и со списками, и с кортежами
        PyObject* x_obj = PySequence_GetItem(point, 0);  // new reference
        PyObject* y_obj = PySequence_GetItem(point, 1);
        PyObject* z_obj = PySequence_GetItem(point, 2);
        
        if (!x_obj || !y_obj || !z_obj) {
            Py_XDECREF(x_obj);
            Py_XDECREF(y_obj);
            Py_XDECREF(z_obj);
            PyErr_SetString(PyExc_RuntimeError, "Failed to get coordinate");
            return NULL;
        }
        
        double x = PyFloat_AsDouble(x_obj);
        double y = PyFloat_AsDouble(y_obj);
        double z = PyFloat_AsDouble(z_obj);
        
        Py_DECREF(x_obj);
        Py_DECREF(y_obj);
        Py_DECREF(z_obj);
        
        // PyFloat_AsDouble возвращает -1.0 при ошибке
        if (x == -1.0 && PyErr_Occurred()) return NULL;
        if (y == -1.0 && PyErr_Occurred()) return NULL;
        if (z == -1.0 && PyErr_Occurred()) return NULL;
        
        points_in.push_back(PT(x, y, z));
    }
    
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> vws;
    std::vector<int> t_add;
    
    try {
        LinPhysER_general_with_points_C(L, d, points_in, vws, t_add,
                                         (bool)check_node_node, (bool)check_node_link);
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        PyErr_Format(PyExc_RuntimeError, "C++ exception: %s", e.what());
        return NULL;
    } catch (...) {
        PyErr_SetString(PyExc_RuntimeError, "Unknown C++ exception");
        return NULL;
    }
    
    // Возвращаем только связи и время добавления (точки уже есть в Python)
    PyObject* python_vws = PyList_New(vws.size());
    for (size_t e = 0; e < vws.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_vws, e, Py_BuildValue("(ii)", vws[e][0], vws[e][1]));
    
    PyObject* python_t_add = PyList_New(t_add.size());
    for (size_t e = 0; e < t_add.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_t_add, e, PyLong_FromLong(t_add[e]));
    
    PyObject* return_tuple = Py_BuildValue("(OO)", python_vws, python_t_add);
    
    Py_DECREF(python_vws);
    Py_DECREF(python_t_add);
    
    return return_tuple;
}

static PyObject* AllToAllSegmentSquaredDist(PyObject* self, PyObject* args){
    int N;
    double maxsd;

    if (!PyArg_ParseTuple(args, "id", &N, &maxsd))
        return NULL;

    std::vector<PT> points;
    points.reserve(N);
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> vws;
    std::vector<std::array<int,2>> metaedges;
    std::vector<double> squareddists;

    AllToAllSegmentSquaredDist_C(N, maxsd, points, vws, metaedges, squareddists);

    PyObject* python_points = PyList_New(points.size());
    for (int v = 0; v < (int)points.size(); v++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_points, v, Py_BuildValue("(fff)", points[v].x(), points[v].y(), points[v].z()));

    PyObject* python_vws = PyList_New(vws.size());
    for (int e = 0; e < (int)vws.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_vws, e, Py_BuildValue("(ii)", vws[e][0], vws[e][1]));

    PyObject* python_metaedges = PyList_New(metaedges.size());
    for (int e = 0; e < (int)metaedges.size(); e++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_metaedges, e, Py_BuildValue("(ii)", metaedges[e][0], metaedges[e][1]));

    PyObject* python_squareddists = PyList_New(squareddists.size());
    for (int x = 0; x < (int)squareddists.size(); x++)
        PyList_SetItem(python_squareddists, x, Py_BuildValue("f", squareddists[x]));

    PyObject* return_tuple = Py_BuildValue("(OOOO)", python_points, python_vws, python_metaedges, python_squareddists);

    Py_DECREF(python_points);
    Py_DECREF(python_vws);
    Py_DECREF(python_metaedges);
    Py_DECREF(python_squareddists);

    return return_tuple;
}

static PyMethodDef mainMethods[] = {
    {"LinPhysER", LinPhysER, METH_VARARGS, "Generate linear physical ER"},
    {"LinPhysER_linkonly", LinPhysER_linkonly, METH_VARARGS, "Generate linear physical ER, link-link interaction only"},
    {"LinPhysER_general", LinPhysER_general, METH_VARARGS, "Generate LPN with configurable physical constraints"},
    {"LinPhysER_general_with_points", LinPhysER_general_with_points, METH_VARARGS, "Generate LPN with external points and configurable physical constraints"},
    {"AllToAllSegmentSquaredDist", AllToAllSegmentSquaredDist, METH_VARARGS, "Drop N random points and calculate the distance between all segment pairs"},
    {NULL, NULL, 0, NULL}
};

static PyModuleDef randLPN_CPP = {
    PyModuleDef_HEAD_INIT,
    "randLPN_CPP", "randLPN_CPP",
    -1,
    mainMethods
};

PyMODINIT_FUNC PyInit_randLPN_CPP(void){
    return PyModule_Create(&randLPN_CPP);
}

from setuptools import setup, Extension
import numpy as np
import sys
import os
import platform

# Пути к CGAL
include_dirs = [np.get_include()]
library_dirs = []

# Вариант 1: CGAL через conda
conda_prefix = os.environ.get('CONDA_PREFIX', '')
if conda_prefix:
    if platform.system() == 'Windows':
        include_dirs.append(os.path.join(conda_prefix, 'Library', 'include'))
        library_dirs.append(os.path.join(conda_prefix, 'Library', 'lib'))
    else:
        include_dirs.append(os.path.join(conda_prefix, 'include'))
        library_dirs.append(os.path.join(conda_prefix, 'lib'))

# Вариант 2: CGAL через vcpkg
vcpkg_root = os.environ.get('VCPKG_ROOT', '')
if vcpkg_root:
    if platform.system() == 'Windows':
        include_dirs.append(os.path.join(vcpkg_root, 'installed', 'x64-windows', 'include'))
        library_dirs.append(os.path.join(vcpkg_root, 'installed', 'x64-windows', 'lib'))

# Определяем флаги компиляции в зависимости от платформы
if platform.system() == 'Windows':
    # MSVC (Visual Studio)
    compile_args = ['/std:c++17', '/O2', '/DCGAL_HEADER_ONLY', '/EHsc']
    link_args = []
else:
    # GCC/Clang (Linux/macOS)
    compile_args = ['-std=c++17', '-O3', '-DCGAL_HEADER_ONLY']
    link_args = []

ext_modules = [
    Extension(
        'randLPN_CPP',
        sources=['randLPN_CPP.cpp'],
        include_dirs=include_dirs,
        library_dirs=library_dirs,
        libraries=['gmp', 'mpfr'] if platform.system() != 'Windows' else [],
        extra_compile_args=compile_args,
        extra_link_args=link_args,
        language='c++',
    )
]

setup(
    name='randLPN_CPP',
    version='1.0.0',
    description='C++ module for LPN generation',
    ext_modules=ext_modules,
)

# ФАЙЛ [15/36]: frontend\src\App.tsx
# Размер: 9241 символов, 295 строк
######################################################################

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Network, Experiment } from './types';
import { NetworkGeneratorTab } from './components/NetworkGeneratorTab';
import { NetworkAnalyzerTab } from './components/NetworkAnalyzerTab';
import { LpnExperimentsTab } from './components/LpnExperimentsTab';

export default function App() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'generator' | 'analyzer' | 'experiments'>('generator');
  const [networks, setNetworks] = useState<Network[]>([]);
  const [experiments, setExperiments] = useState<Experiment[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const netRes = await fetch('/api/networks');
      if (netRes.ok) {
        const netData = await netRes.json();
        setNetworks(netData);
      }
      
      const expRes = await fetch('/api/experiments');
      if (expRes.ok) {
        const expData = await expRes.json();
        setExperiments(expData);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to connect to backend api:', err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData();
  }, [activeTab]);

  const handleGenerateNetwork = async (formData: any) => {
    if (formData === null) {
      setIsLoading(true);
      await fetchData();
      setIsLoading(false);
      return null;
    }
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/generate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(formData),
      });
      
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.detail || 'Failed to generate network');
      }
      
      await fetchData();
      setIsLoading(false);
      return data;
    } catch (err) {
      setIsLoading(false);
      throw err;
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteNetwork = async (id: number) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/networks/${id}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        setNetworks((prev) => prev.filter((n) => n.id !== id));
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to delete network:', err);
    }
  };

  const [analysisProgress, setAnalysisProgress] = useState<{ current: number; total: number; message: string } | null>(null);
  const [experimentProgress, setExperimentProgress] = useState<{ current: number; total: number; message: string } | null>(null);

  const readProgressStream = async (
    res: Response,
    onProgress: (progress: { current: number; total: number; message: string }) => void,
    onSuccess: (data: any) => void
  ) => {
    const reader = res.body?.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
    let buffer = '';
    
    if (reader) {
      while (true) {
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const lines = buffer.split('\n');
        buffer = lines.pop() || '';
        
        for (const line of lines) {
          if (line.trim()) {
            try {
              const step = JSON.parse(line);
              if (step.type === 'progress') {
                onProgress({
                  current: (step.index ?? 0) + 1,
                  total: step.total ?? 100,
                  message: step.message || '',
                });
              } else if (step.type === 'success') {
                onSuccess(step.results);
              }
            } catch (err) {
              console.error('Error parsing stream line:', err);
            }
          }
        }
      }
      if (buffer.trim()) {
        try {
          const step = JSON.parse(buffer);
          if (step.type === 'success') {
            onSuccess(step.results);
          }
        } catch (err) {}
      }
    }
  };

  const handleAnalyze = async (networkIds: number[], checkboxes: string[], threads: number) => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setAnalysisProgress({ current: 0, total: networkIds.length, message: 'Инициализация анализа свойств...' });
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/analyze', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          network_ids: networkIds,
          checkboxes,
          threads,
        }),
      });
      
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(await res.text() || 'Failed to analyze');
      }

      await readProgressStream(
        res,
        (prog) => setAnalysisProgress(prog),
        (results) => {}
      );
      
      await fetchData();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to analyze:', err);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setAnalysisProgress(null);
    }
  };

  const handleAnalyzeAllUnanalyzed = async (checkboxes: string[], threads: number) => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setAnalysisProgress({ current: 0, total: 100, message: 'Подготовка общего анализа нерассчитанных свойств...' });
    try {
      const queryParams = checkboxes.map((col) => `checkboxes=${col}`).join('&');
      const res = await fetch(`/api/analyze-all-unanalyzed?${queryParams}&threads=${threads}`, {
        method: 'POST',
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(await res.text() || 'Failed to analyze uncalculated');
      }

      await readProgressStream(
        res,
        (prog) => setAnalysisProgress(prog),
        (results) => {}
      );

      await fetchData();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to analyze all uncalculated:', err);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setAnalysisProgress(null);
    }
  };

  const handleRunExperiment = async (formData: any) => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setExperimentProgress({ current: 0, total: 100, message: 'Запуск серий экспериментов...' });
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/run_experiment', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(formData),
      });
      
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(await res.text() || 'Failed to run experiment');
      }

      let finalResults: any = null;
      await readProgressStream(
        res,
        (prog) => setExperimentProgress(prog),
        (results) => {
          finalResults = results;
        }
      );
      
      await fetchData();
      setIsLoading(false);
      setExperimentProgress(null);
      return finalResults;
    } catch (err) {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setExperimentProgress(null);
      throw err;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#f4f7f6] text-[#2c3e50] font-sans p-4 sm:p-8">
      <nav className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-6 bg-[#2c3e50] p-3 rounded-xl shadow-md font-sans">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('generator')}
          className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs uppercase font-bold cursor-pointer tracking-wider rounded-lg transition-all duration-150 border-none ${
            activeTab === 'generator'
              ? 'bg-[#3498db] text-white shadow-sm'
              : 'text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#34495e] hover:text-white bg-transparent'
          }`}
        >
          1. Генератор
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('analyzer')}
          className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs uppercase font-bold cursor-pointer tracking-wider rounded-lg transition-all duration-150 border-none ${
            activeTab === 'analyzer'
              ? 'bg-[#3498db] text-white shadow-sm'
              : 'text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#34495e] hover:text-white bg-transparent'
          }`}
        >
          2. Анализ
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('experiments')}
          className={`px-4 py-2 text-xs uppercase font-bold cursor-pointer tracking-wider rounded-lg transition-all duration-150 border-none ${
            activeTab === 'experiments'
              ? 'bg-[#3498db] text-white shadow-sm'
              : 'text-gray-300 hover:bg-[#34495e] hover:text-white bg-transparent'
          }`}
        >
          3. Изучение заторов
        </button>
      </nav>

      <main className="flex-grow bg-white border border-[#eee] p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
        {activeTab === 'generator' && (
          <NetworkGeneratorTab
            networks={networks}
            isLoading={isLoading}
            onGenerate={handleGenerateNetwork}
            onDelete={handleDeleteNetwork}
          />
        )}
        {activeTab === 'analyzer' && (
          <NetworkAnalyzerTab
            networks={networks}
            isLoading={isLoading}
            onAnalyze={handleAnalyze}
            onAnalyzeAllUnanalyzed={handleAnalyzeAllUnanalyzed}
            progress={analysisProgress}
            onSaveAveragedCompleted={fetchData}
          />
        )}
        {activeTab === 'experiments' && (
          <LpnExperimentsTab
            experiments={experiments}
            isLoading={isLoading}
            onRunExperiment={handleRunExperiment}
            progress={experimentProgress}
          />
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


######################################################################
# ФАЙЛ [16/36]: frontend\src\components\CompareDegreeDistributions.tsx
# Размер: 18793 символов, 468 строк
######################################################################

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Network, ComparisonProfile, matchNetworkWithProfile } from '../types';
import { RefreshCw, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { InfoTooltip } from './InfoTooltip';

interface CompareDegreeDistributionsProps {
  networks: Network[];
  profiles: ComparisonProfile[];
}

interface NetworkDetails {
  id: number;
  nodes: number[][];
  links: number[][];
}

export const CompareDegreeDistributions: React.FC<CompareDegreeDistributionsProps> = ({
  networks,
  profiles,
}) => {
  const [cache, setCache] = useState<Record<number, NetworkDetails>>({});
  const [loadingIds, setLoadingIds] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [displayMode, setDisplayMode] = useState<'profiles' | 'networks'>('profiles');
  const [hoveredK, setHoveredK] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const enabledProfiles = profiles.filter((p) => p.enabled);
  const activeNetworks = networks.filter((net) =>
    enabledProfiles.some((prof) => matchNetworkWithProfile(net, prof))
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    activeNetworks.forEach((net) => {
      const id = net.id;
      if (!cache[id] && !loadingIds.includes(id)) {
        setLoadingIds((prev) => [...prev, id]);
        fetch(`/api/networks/${id}/details`)
          .then((res) => {
            if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Failed to load network ${id} details`);
            return res.json();
          })
          .then((data: NetworkDetails) => {
            setCache((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: data }));
            setLoadingIds((prev) => prev.filter((loadingId) => loadingId !== id));
          })
          .catch((err) => {
            console.error(`Error loading network details for ${id}:`, err);
            setLoadingIds((prev) => prev.filter((loadingId) => loadingId !== id));
          });
      }
    });
  }, [activeNetworks, cache, loadingIds]);

  const getNetworkDistribution = (net: Network) => {
    const details = cache[net.id];
    if (!details) return [];
    const n = net.N || details.nodes.length;
    if (n === 0) return [];
    const degrees = new Array(n).fill(0);
    details.links.forEach(([u, v]) => {
      if (u >= 0 && u < n) degrees[u]++;
      if (v >= 0 && v < n) degrees[v]++;
    });

    const counts: Record<number, number> = {};
    degrees.forEach((d) => {
      counts[d] = (counts[d] || 0) + 1;
    });

    const maxDeg = Math.max(...degrees, 0);
    const distribution: { k: number; p: number }[] = [];
    for (let k = 0; k <= maxDeg; k++) {
      const c = counts[k] || 0;
      distribution.push({ k, p: c / n });
    }
    return distribution;
  };

  const netDistributions: Record<number, { k: number; p: number }[]> = {};
  let maxDegreeGlobal = 0;

  activeNetworks.forEach((net) => {
    if (!cache[net.id]) return;
    const dist = getNetworkDistribution(net);
    netDistributions[net.id] = dist;
    if (dist.length - 1 > maxDegreeGlobal) {
      maxDegreeGlobal = dist.length - 1;
    }
  });

  interface CurveData {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    profileName: string;
    color: string;
    distribution: { k: number; p: number }[];
    isProfileAverage: boolean;
    networkCount?: number;
  }

  const curves: CurveData[] = [];

  if (displayMode === 'profiles') {
    enabledProfiles.forEach((prof) => {
      const matchedAndCached = activeNetworks.filter(
        (net) => matchNetworkWithProfile(net, prof) && cache[net.id]
      );
      if (matchedAndCached.length === 0) return;

      const avgDistribution: { k: number; p: number }[] = [];
      for (let k = 0; k <= maxDegreeGlobal; k++) {
        let sumP = 0;
        let count = 0;
        matchedAndCached.forEach((net) => {
          const dist = netDistributions[net.id];
          if (dist) {
            const item = dist.find((d) => d.k === k);
            if (item) {
              sumP += item.p;
              count++;
            } else if (k <= dist.length - 1) {
              count++;
            }
          }
        });
        if (count > 0) {
          avgDistribution.push({ k, p: sumP / matchedAndCached.length });
        }
      }

      curves.push({
        id: prof.id,
        name: `${prof.name} (среднее)`,
        profileName: prof.name,
        color: prof.color,
        distribution: avgDistribution,
        isProfileAverage: true,
        networkCount: matchedAndCached.length,
      });
    });
  } else {
    enabledProfiles.forEach((prof) => {
      const matchedAndCached = activeNetworks.filter(
        (net) => matchNetworkWithProfile(net, prof) && cache[net.id]
      );
      matchedAndCached.forEach((net) => {
        const dist = netDistributions[net.id];
        if (!dist) return;

        curves.push({
          id: `${prof.id}-${net.id}`,
          name: `${prof.name} • ${net.name}`,
          profileName: prof.name,
          color: prof.color,
          distribution: dist,
          isProfileAverage: false,
        });
      });
    });
  }

  let maxPGlobal = 0.05;
  curves.forEach((c) => {
    c.distribution.forEach((item) => {
      if (item.p > maxPGlobal) maxPGlobal = item.p;
    });
  });
  maxPGlobal = Math.max(0.1, Math.ceil(maxPGlobal * 10) / 10);

  const width = 600;
  const height = 280;
  const paddingLeft = 45;
  const paddingRight = 20;
  const paddingTop = 30;
  const paddingBottom = 40;

  const chartWidth = width - paddingLeft - paddingRight;
  const chartHeight = height - paddingTop - paddingBottom;

  const getX = (k: number) => {
    if (maxDegreeGlobal === 0) return paddingLeft;
    return paddingLeft + (k / maxDegreeGlobal) * chartWidth;
  };

  const getY = (p: number) => {
    return paddingTop + chartHeight * (1 - p / maxPGlobal);
  };

  const makePathD = (dist: { k: number; p: number }[]) => {
    const coordinates = dist.map((item) => `${getX(item.k)},${getY(item.p)}`);
    return coordinates.length > 0 ? `M ${coordinates.join(' L ')}` : '';
  };

  const makeAreaPathD = (dist: { k: number; p: number }[]) => {
    if (dist.length === 0) return '';
    const points = dist.map((item) => `${getX(item.k)},${getY(item.p)}`);
    const firstX = getX(dist[0].k);
    const lastX = getX(dist[dist.length - 1].k);
    const bottomY = paddingTop + chartHeight;
    return `M ${firstX},${bottomY} L ${points.join(' L ')} L ${lastX},${bottomY} Z`;
  };

  const xTicks: number[] = [];
  const stepX = maxDegreeGlobal <= 10 ? 1 : maxDegreeGlobal <= 25 ? 2 : maxDegreeGlobal <= 100 ? 10 : 20;
  for (let k = 0; k <= maxDegreeGlobal; k += stepX) {
    xTicks.push(k);
  }
  if (!xTicks.includes(maxDegreeGlobal)) {
    xTicks.push(maxDegreeGlobal);
  }

  const yTicks = [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map((r) => r * maxPGlobal);
  const isSomeLoading = loadingIds.length > 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 shadow-sm rounded-xl p-5 mt-6 font-sans">
      <div className="border-b border-gray-150 pb-3 mb-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center gap-3">
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#2c3e50] uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span>Сравнение распределений степеней вершин P(k)</span>
            <InfoTooltip text="Показывает долю вершин, радиально соединенных с k рёбрами. Выявляет пуассоновскую топологию (ER/LPN при малых λ) или степенной безмасштабный закон (BA)." />
          </h3>
          <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 italic mt-0.5">
            Совмещение кривых распределения для различных сетевых топологий на одной масштабируемой гистограмме.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={() => setDisplayMode('profiles')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase rounded-md border-0 cursor-pointer ${
              displayMode === 'profiles'
                ? 'bg-white text-gray-800 shadow-xs'
                : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 bg-transparent'
            }`}
          >
            Среднее по профилям
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setDisplayMode('networks')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase rounded-md border-0 cursor-pointer ${
              displayMode === 'networks'
                ? 'bg-white text-gray-800 shadow-xs'
                : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 bg-transparent'
            }`}
          >
            Все сети раздельно
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
        <div className="lg:col-span-4 flex flex-col justify-between border-r border-gray-100 pr-0 lg:pr-5">
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 tracking-wider block">Активные кривые</span>
            
            {enabledProfiles.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 italic">Нет активных (отмеченных флажком) профилей сравнения.</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-[220px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                {enabledProfiles.map((prof) => {
                  const matched = activeNetworks.filter((n) => matchNetworkWithProfile(n, prof));
                  const loadedCount = matched.filter((n) => cache[n.id]).length;
                  const loadingCount = matched.length - loadedCount;

                  return (
                    <div
                      key={prof.id}
                      className="border border-gray-200 p-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-50/50 flex flex-col gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span
                          className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full block shrink-0"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: prof.color }}
                        />
                        <span className="text-gray-800 truncate" title={prof.name}>
                          {prof.name}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-[9px] font-mono text-gray-400 font-semibold pl-4 font-bold">
                        <span>Сетей в БД: {matched.length}</span>
                        {loadingCount > 0 ? (
                          <span className="text-blue-500 animate-pulse">Загрузка {loadedCount}/{matched.length}...</span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-gray-500 text-[10px] flex items-center gap-0.5">
                            <Check className="h-3 w-3 text-green-500" /> Готово
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {isSomeLoading && (
            <div className="mt-3 flex items-center font-mono font-bold text-[10px] text-blue-500 gap-1 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-100 p-2 rounded-lg shrink-0">
              <RefreshCw className="animate-spin h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>Фоновая подгрузка реберных массивов из SQLite...</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
          {enabledProfiles.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-gray-400 italic text-xs h-64 border border-dashed border-gray-200 rounded-xl bg-gray-50/50 w-full col-span-3">
              Включите хотя бы один настроенный профиль в меню управления выше.
            </div>
          ) : activeNetworks.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-gray-400 italic text-xs h-64 border border-dashed border-gray-200 rounded-xl bg-gray-50/50 w-full col-span-3">
              Нет сетей в базе данных под критерии выбранных профилей. Сгенерируйте их во вкладке ГЕНЕРАЦИЯ.
            </div>
          ) : curves.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-blue-500 text-xs h-64 border border-dashed border-gray-200 rounded-xl bg-gray-50/50 w-full font-mono font-bold col-span-3">
              <RefreshCw className="animate-spin h-6 w-6 mb-2 text-blue-500" /> Загрузка графов и весов степеней из SQLite...
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full relative">
              <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} className="w-full h-auto overflow-visible select-none">
                <defs>
                  <clipPath id="pk-compare-clip">
                    <rect x={paddingLeft} y={paddingTop} width={chartWidth} height={chartHeight} />
                  </clipPath>
                </defs>

                {yTicks.map((yVal, idx) => {
                  const y = getY(yVal);
                  return (
                    <g key={idx}>
                      <line
                        x1={paddingLeft}
                        y1={y}
                        x2={width - paddingRight}
                        y2={y}
                        stroke="#f0f0f0"
                        strokeWidth="1"
                      />
                      <text
                        x={paddingLeft - 8}
                        y={y + 3}
                        textAnchor="end"
                        className="fill-gray-400 font-mono text-[9px] font-bold"
                      >
                        {(yVal * 100).toFixed(0)}%
                      </text>
                    </g>
                  );
                })}

                {xTicks.map((kVal, idx) => {
                  const x = getX(kVal);
                  return (
                    <g key={idx}>
                      <line
                        x1={x}
                        y1={paddingTop}
                        x2={x}
                        y2={paddingTop + chartHeight}
                        stroke="#f5f5f5"
                        strokeWidth="1"
                      />
                      <text
                        x={x}
                        y={paddingTop + chartHeight + 14}
                        textAnchor="middle"
                        className="fill-gray-400 font-mono text-[9px] font-bold"
                      >
                        k={kVal}
                      </text>
                    </g>
                  );
                })}

                <line
                  x1={paddingLeft}
                  y1={paddingTop + chartHeight}
                  x2={width - paddingRight}
                  y2={paddingTop + chartHeight}
                  stroke="#ccc"
                  strokeWidth="1.5"
                />
                <line
                  x1={paddingLeft}
                  y1={paddingTop}
                  x2={paddingLeft}
                  y2={paddingTop + chartHeight}
                  stroke="#ccc"
                  strokeWidth="1.5"
                />

                <g clipPath="url(#pk-compare-clip)">
                  {curves.map((curve) => {
                    const pathD = makePathD(curve.distribution);
                    const areaD = makeAreaPathD(curve.distribution);

                    return (
                      <g key={curve.id}>
                        <path
                          d={areaD}
                          fill={curve.color}
                          fillOpacity={displayMode === 'profiles' ? 0.15 : 0.05}
                          className="transition-all duration-300"
                        />
                        <path
                          d={pathD}
                          fill="none"
                          stroke={curve.color}
                          strokeWidth={curve.isProfileAverage ? 3 : 1.8}
                          strokeLinecap="round"
                          strokeLinejoin="round"
                          strokeDasharray={curve.isProfileAverage ? "0" : "5,3"}
                          className="transition-all duration-300"
                        />
                        {curve.distribution.map((item, pIdx) => {
                          const cx = getX(item.k);
                          const cy = getY(item.p);
                          const isKHovered = hoveredK === item.k;
                          return (
                            <circle
                              key={`${curve.id}-dot-${pIdx}`}
                              cx={cx}
                              cy={cy}
                              r={isKHovered ? 5 : curve.isProfileAverage ? 3.5 : 2.5}
                              fill={curve.color}
                              stroke="#fff"
                              strokeWidth={isKHovered ? 2 : 1}
                              onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredK(item.k)}
                              onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredK(null)}
                              className="cursor-pointer transition-all duration-150"
                            />
                          );
                        })}
                      </g>
                    );
                  })}
                </g>
              </svg>

              <div className="h-10 mt-2.5 flex justify-center items-center font-mono text-[9px] text-gray-600 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-150 rounded-lg px-3">
                {hoveredK !== null ? (
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3">
                    <span>Степень <strong className="text-gray-950 font-bold text-[10px]">k = {hoveredK}</strong></span>
                    <span className="text-gray-300">|</span>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 items-center">
                      {curves.slice(0, 5).map((curve) => {
                        const prob = curve.distribution.find((d) => d.k === hoveredK)?.p || 0;
                        return (
                          <span key={curve.id} style={{ color: curve.color }} className="font-bold">
                            {curve.name}: {(prob * 100).toFixed(1)}%
                          </span>
                        );
                      })}
                      {curves.length > 5 && <span className="text-gray-400 italic">и ещё {curves.length - 5} сетей</span>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-gray-400 italic text-center leading-tight">
                    Наведите курсор мыши на маркеры на графике, чтобы в реальном времени считать точную удельную долю вершин P(k) при выбранной степени k.
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

# ФАЙЛ [17/36]: frontend\src\components\DegreeDistributionChart.tsx
# Размер: 6370 символов, 177 строк
######################################################################

import React, { useState } from 'react';

interface DegreeDistributionChartProps {
  nodesCount: number;
  links: number[][];
}

export const DegreeDistributionChart: React.FC<DegreeDistributionChartProps> = ({
  nodesCount,
  links,
}) => {
  const [hoveredIndex, setHoveredIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

  if (nodesCount <= 0) return null;

  const degrees = new Array(nodesCount).fill(0);
  links.forEach(([u, v]) => {
    if (u >= 0 && u < nodesCount) degrees[u]++;
    if (v >= 0 && v < nodesCount) degrees[v]++;
  });

  const freq: Record<number, number> = {};
  degrees.forEach((d) => {
    freq[d] = (freq[d] || 0) + 1;
  });

  const maxDegree = Math.max(...degrees, 0);
  const data: Array<{ degree: number; count: number; freq: number }> = [];
  for (let d = 0; d <= maxDegree; d++) {
    const count = freq[d] || 0;
    data.push({
      degree: d,
      count,
      freq: count / nodesCount,
    });
  }

  const isolatedCount = freq[0] || 0;
  const avgDegree = degrees.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d, 0) / (nodesCount || 1);
  const peakDegreeItem = [...data].sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)[0];

  const width = 500;
  const height = 240;
  const paddingLeft = 45;
  const paddingRight = 20;
  const paddingTop = 25;
  const paddingBottom = 40;

  const chartWidth = width - paddingLeft - paddingRight;
  const chartHeight = height - paddingTop - paddingBottom;

  const maxFreq = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.freq), 0.01);

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-sm mt-6">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center border-b border-gray-100 pb-3 mb-4 gap-2">
        <div>
          <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-800 uppercase tracking-wider">
            Распределение степеней вершин P(k)
          </h4>
          <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 italic mt-0.5">
            Статистическая вероятность наличия k связей у случайного узла сети.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono">
          <span className="bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 px-2 py-1 rounded text-gray-600">
            Ср. степень: <strong>{avgDegree.toFixed(2)}</strong>
          </span>
          <span className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-100 px-2 py-1 rounded text-blue-700">
            Пик (k): <strong>{peakDegreeItem ? peakDegreeItem.degree : 0}</strong>
          </span>
          <span className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100 px-2 py-1 rounded text-amber-700">
            Изолированных (k=0): <strong>{isolatedCount}</strong>
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="relative">
        <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} className="w-full h-auto overflow-visible select-none">
          {[0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map((ratio, idx) => {
            const hVal = (maxFreq * ratio).toFixed(3);
            const y = paddingTop + chartHeight * (1 - ratio);
            return (
              <g key={idx}>
                <line
                  x1={paddingLeft}
                  y1={y}
                  x2={width - paddingRight}
                  y2={y}
                  stroke="#f0f0f0"
                  strokeWidth="1"
                  strokeDasharray="4,4"
                />
                <text
                  x={paddingLeft - 8}
                  y={y + 3}
                  textAnchor="end"
                  className="fill-gray-400 font-mono text-[9px] font-bold"
                >
                  {hVal}
                </text>
              </g>
            );
          })}

          {data.map((item, idx) => {
            const barWidth = Math.max(2, Math.min(30, (chartWidth / (data.length || 1)) - 4));
            const x = paddingLeft + (chartWidth / (data.length || 1)) * idx + (chartWidth / (data.length || 1) - barWidth) / 2;
            const barHeight = (item.freq / maxFreq) * chartHeight;
            const y = paddingTop + chartHeight - barHeight;

            return (
              <g
                key={idx}
                onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredIndex(idx)}
                onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredIndex(null)}
                className="cursor-pointer group"
              >
                <rect
                  x={x}
                  y={y}
                  width={barWidth}
                  height={barHeight}
                  fill={hoveredIndex === idx ? '#3498db' : '#85c1e9'}
                  rx="2"
                  className="transition-colors duration-200"
                />

                <rect
                  x={x - 2}
                  y={paddingTop}
                  width={barWidth + 4}
                  height={chartHeight}
                  fill="transparent"
                />

                {(data.length <= 15 || idx % Math.ceil(data.length / 10) === 0 || hoveredIndex === idx) && (
                  <text
                    x={x + barWidth / 2}
                    y={paddingTop + chartHeight + 14}
                    textAnchor="middle"
                    className={`font-mono text-[9px] ${
                      hoveredIndex === idx ? 'fill-blue-600 font-bold' : 'fill-gray-500'
                    }`}
                  >
                    k={item.degree}
                  </text>
                )}
              </g>
            );
          })}

          <line
            x1={paddingLeft}
            y1={paddingTop + chartHeight}
            x2={width - paddingRight}
            y2={paddingTop + chartHeight}
            stroke="#ccc"
            strokeWidth="1.5"
          />
        </svg>

        <div className="h-6 mt-1 flex justify-center items-center font-mono text-[10px] text-gray-600 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-100 rounded-lg py-1">
          {hoveredIndex !== null ? (
            <span>
              Степень <strong className="text-blue-600 font-bold">k = {data[hoveredIndex].degree}</strong>: количество вершин ={' '}
              <strong className="text-gray-900 font-bold">{data[hoveredIndex].count}</strong> ({' '}
              {(data[hoveredIndex].freq * 100).toFixed(1)}% )
            </span>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-gray-400 italic">Наведите курсор на столбец распределения, чтобы узнать детали</span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

interface InfoTooltipProps {
  text: string;
}

export const InfoTooltip: React.FC<InfoTooltipProps> = ({ text }) => {
  return (
    <span className="relative group inline-block ml-1.5 align-middle text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 transition-colors cursor-help">
      <HelpCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
      <span className="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 mb-2 w-52 p-2.5 text-[10px] leading-normal bg-[#2c3e50] text-white rounded-lg shadow-xl opacity-0 pointer-events-none group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-200 z-[9999] text-center font-medium normal-case">
        {text}
        <span className="absolute top-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 border-opacity-100 border-[6px] border-transparent border-t-[#2c3e50]" />
